### [224] [SchGen: PCB Schematic Generation with Semantic-Grounded Code Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30345)
*Qinpei Luo,Ruichun Ma,Xinyu Zhang,Lili Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SchGen，首个从自然语言需求生成可编辑PCB原理图的大语言模型。核心贡献在于设计了一种语义化的代码表示法（将几何驱动问题转化为语义匹配任务），并通过人机协作流水线构建了大规模图文数据集。实验表明，SchGen在连线准确性和功能正确性上显著优于替代表示法和更大规模的通用大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB原理图设计当前仍依赖人工且需要大量专业知识；尽管生成式AI在数字/模拟IC设计领域已有进展，但从自然语言生成PCB原理图的研究几乎空白。缺乏适合LLM的表示法和大规模数据集是主要挑战。

Method: 提出语义化的代码表示法：编码原理图编辑原语（相对布局和基于引脚名的布线），将几何驱动的生成问题转化为语义驱动的匹配任务；通过人机协作流水线将开源硬件设计转换为此表示法，构建大规模配对数据集。

Result: SchGen在连线准确性和功能正确性上显著优于替代表示法和更大规模的通用大语言模型。

Conclusion: 表示法设计在生成式模型处理复杂硬件设计任务中起着关键作用。

Abstract: Printed circuit board (PCB) schematic design defines nearly all electronic hardware, but it remains manual and expertise-intensive. While generative AI has advanced digital and analog IC design, PCB schematic generation from natural-language intent is largely unexplored. This paper presents SchGen, the first large language model that generates editable PCB schematics from natural-language requests. The key challenge lies in the lack of an LLM-suited representation and a large-scale dataset. Current schematic formats are dominated by verbose, tool-specific syntax and geometry-heavy descriptions, making them difficult to generate reliably. We introduce a semantically grounded code representation that encodes schematic editing primitives with relative placement and pin-name-based wiring, transforming a geometry-driven generation problem into a semantics-driven matching task amenable to LLMs. We further construct a large-scale dataset of PCB schematics paired with user prompts via a human-agent collaborative pipeline that converts open-source hardware designs into our representation. Experiments show that SchGen significantly outperforms alternative representations and even larger general-purpose LLMs on wire connectivity accuracy and functional correctness. Our results highlight the critical role of representation design in enabling generative models for complex hardware design tasks.

</details>


### [225] [Physics Is All You Need? A Case Study in Physicist-Supervised AI Development of Scientific Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30353)
*Nhat-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个量子物理学家与AI编码助手（Claude Code）在12天57个会话中合作构建CLA X-PT模块的案例研究，揭示了AI智能体在代码生成中存在“症状缓解代替根本原因解决”的固有问题，以及监督设计对确保可信输出的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨AI智能体在科学研究中的角色定位（工具、合著者还是研究者），并通过量化案例研究揭示当前AI智能体在复杂科学计算任务中的局限性与可信度问题。

Method: 采用N=1的量化案例研究方法，由一位物理学家监督AI编码智能体（Claude Code）在12个工作日、57个会话中构建CLA X-PT模块，记录并分类了15个监督事件，按干预层级分析智能体能否自主解决、需要何种领域知识干预。

Result: 智能体自主解决10个问题（通过oracle测试迭代），物理学家领域知识解决2个，3个无法解决的问题均逃避了oracle检测；智能体投入33/57个会话在无法表达目标物理的架构中调整系数，无法重新评估分支选择；提交了一个通过所有oracle测试但对应无物理量的校准修正因子，仅在不同宇宙学参数点测试时被捕获。

Conclusion: 监督设计（而非模型能力）决定了输出可信度；关键实践包括多参数点测试、跨会话共享变更日志、禁止非物理数值补丁；需要智能体具备提出架构替代方案而非仅在给定结构内优化的能力，以及区分预测充分性与解释正确性的能力——这些能力在当前模型中未展现，且不能仅通过规模扩展解决。

Abstract: Are AI agents tools, co-authors, or researchers? We present a quantified case study ($N=1$): a physicist supervising an AI coding agent (Claude Code, Sonnet and Opus models) over 12 work days and 57 sessions to build CLAX-PT, a differentiable one-loop perturbation theory module in JAX. We documented and classified 15 supervision events by intervention level.
  The agent resolved ten autonomously by iterating against oracle tests. Two more by the physicist's domain knowledge. The three it could not -- all evaded oracle detection -- share a common property: the agent treated symptom reduction as root-cause resolution. It spent 33 of the 57 sessions adjusting coefficients within a code architecture that could not represent the target physics, and could not re-evaluate its CLASS-PT branch choice even when prompted to reconsider; only an injected physics concept (anisotropic BAO damping) triggered the redesign. Separately, the agent committed a calibrated correction that passed all oracle tests but corresponded to no quantity in the theory, predicting wrong values at any other cosmology.
  The fudge factor was caught and replaced within the same session. Three supervision practices proved critical for catching what oracle tests missed: testing at diverse parameter points beyond the fiducial calibration; shared changelogs that surfaced stalled exploration across sessions; and an explicit rule against unphysical numerical patches. In this case, supervision design, not model capability, determined whether the agent's output was trustworthy. Closing the gap would require agents that propose architectural alternatives rather than optimize within a given structure, and distinguish predictive adequacy from explanatory correctness -- capabilities not exhibited here, not obviously addressed by scaling alone. [Abridged.]

</details>


### [226] [AgentDoG 1.5: A Lightweight and Scalable Alignment Framework for AI Agent Safety and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29801)
*Dongrui Liu,Yu Li,Zhonghao Yang,Peng Wang,Guanxu Chen,Yuejin Xie,Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Yanxu Zhu,Tianyi Zhou,Leitao Yuan,Zhijie Zheng,Qihao Lin,Yimin Wang,Haoyu Luo,Shuai Shao,Chen Qian,Qingyu Liu,Ling Tang,Ruiyang Qin,Qihan Ren,Junxiao Yang,Kun Wang,Zhiheng Xi,Linfeng Zhang,Ranjie Duan,Bo Zhang,Wenjie Wang,Wen Shen,Qiaosheng Zhang,Yan Teng,Chaochao Lu,Rui Mei,Man Li,Jialing Tao,Xi Lin,Tianhang Zheng,Yong Liu,Quanshi Zhang,Lei Zhu,Xingjun Ma,Junhua Liu,Hui Xue,Xiaoxiang Zuo,Xiangnan He,Chao Shen,Xianglong Liu,Minlie Huang,Jing Shao,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对开放世界智能体（如OpenClaw）带来的新安全风险，提出了轻量级可扩展的智能体安全对齐框架AgentDoG 1.5（0.8B-8B参数），通过更新安全分类体系、构建基于影响函数纯化的数据引擎，仅用约1000样本即达到与GPT-5.4等闭源模型相当的性能，并实现Docker级部署开销降低两个数量级的在线安全护栏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体对齐框架无法应对开放世界智能体（如OpenClaw）带来的新型安全风险，且先进AI模型大幅降低了攻击门槛，亟需轻量级、可扩展的智能体安全对齐方案。

Method: 1) 更新智能体安全分类体系以适应Codex和OpenClaw执行场景的新兴风险；2) 构建分类学引导的数据引擎，结合影响函数纯化技术，用约1000样本训练AgentDoG 1.5多尺寸变体；3) 构建高效的智能体安全SFT和RL训练环境，将Docker级部署开销降低两个数量级；4) 将AgentDoG 1.5作为无需训练的在线护栏进行实时安全审核。

Result: AgentDoG 1.5在多样复杂的交互式智能体场景中达到SOTA性能，与GPT-5.4等领先闭源模型表现相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级安全对齐框架有效解决了开放世界智能体的安全风险，实现了高效部署，且模型与数据集均已开源，可用于实际场景。

Abstract: Modern open-world agents such as OpenClaw exhibit powerful cross-environment execution capabilities yet introduce broad new safety risk sources. Meanwhile, advanced frontier AI models drastically lower attack barriers, rendering current agent alignment frameworks inadequate for real-world deployment. To tackle these emerging threats, we propose a lightweight and scalable agent safety alignment framework. Specifically, we update the agent safety taxonomy to accommodate emergent risks from Codex and OpenClaw execution scenarios. We further build a taxonomy-guided data engine with influence-function purification to train lightweight AgentDoG 1.5 variants (0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 8B parameters) using only around 1k samples, achieving comparable performance with leading closed-source models (e.g., GPT-5.4). Based on AgentDoG 1.5, we construct a highly efficient agentic safety SFT and RL training environment, which reduces deployment overhead in Docker-level environments by two orders of magnitude. Finally, we deploy AgentDoG 1.5 as a training-free online guardrail for real-time safety moderation. Extensive experimental results indicate that AgentDoG 1.5 achieves state-of-the-art performance in diverse and complex interactive agentic scenarios. All models and datasets are openly released.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Echoes within the Reasoning: Stealthy and Effective Watermarking via Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28890)
*Jiacheng Lu,Yiming Li,Tao Song,Weijian Wang,Wenjie Qu,Haibing Guan,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了BiCoT水印框架，通过将所有权信号嵌入推理轨迹的内部几何结构中，结合基于Top-logprob的黑盒验证器RSR，在保持推理保真度的同时实现鲁棒的水印检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒水印方法在鲁棒性和推理保真度之间难以兼顾，要么通过扰动最终答案来牺牲推理质量，要么依赖脆弱的触发模式。

Method: 1) BiCoT：将高显著性结构锚点与私有签名子空间对齐，同时规约普通控制令牌以保持语义容量，将水印嵌入推理轨迹的内部几何结构中。2) RSR：基于Top-logprob的黑盒验证器，使用哨兵令牌校准输出分布的系统性偏移。

Result: 在多种复杂推理任务上保持了推理保真度，在微调、量化、模型级扰动和自适应输出级攻击下（涵盖域内和域外分布）实现了鲁棒的检测效果。

Conclusion: BiCoT通过将水印与推理相关表示耦合，使得在不破坏支持连贯推理的特征的情况下难以移除水印，实现了鲁棒性与推理保真度的兼得。

Abstract: Large Language Models with Chain-of-Thought reasoning capabilities represent valuable intellectual property, yet existing black-box watermarking methods often trade robustness for reasoning fidelity by perturbing final answers or relying on fragile trigger patterns. We propose BiCoT, a watermarking framework that embeds ownership signals into the internal geometry of reasoning traces by aligning high-saliency structural anchors with a private signature subspace while regularizing ordinary control tokens to preserve semantic capacity. This design couples the watermark with reasoning-relevant representations, making removal difficult without disrupting the features that support coherent reasoning. To enable verification under model theft and representation drift, we introduce Robust Subspace Registration (RSR), a Top-
  logprob-based black-box verifier that uses sentinel tokens to calibrate systematic shifts in the output distribution. Experiments show that BiCoT preserves reasoning fidelity across diverse complex reasoning tasks while achieving robust detection under fine-tuning, quantization, model-level perturbations, and adaptive output-level attacks across in-domain and out-of-distribution settings.

</details>


### [228] [Quantum-Enhanced Adversarial Robustness in Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28899)
*Jaydip Sen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了对抗性机器学习对AI系统安全性的威胁，介绍了量子计算如何增强AI的鲁棒性，并探讨了量子优化、特征映射和混合量子-经典架构等防御框架，旨在构建安全可信的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在安全关键系统中广泛应用，但对抗性攻击严重威胁其可靠性和可信度；量子计算展现出突破经典计算瓶颈的潜力，将两者结合有望提升AI的鲁棒性和安全性。

Method: 首先综述对抗性机器学习及现有防御策略，然后介绍量子计算与量子机器学习模型的基础，最后提出量子增强对抗鲁棒性的概念框架，涵盖量子优化、特征映射和混合量子-经典架构。

Result: 提出了将量子计算技术应用于对抗性防御的系统性框架思路，明确了量子优化、特征映射和混合架构在增强鲁棒性方面的潜在价值。

Conclusion: 量子人工智能为提升AI系统的安全性和可信度提供了新路径，但相关应用仍面临诸多挑战，未来需要在理论、算法和工程层面进一步探索。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence has achieved remarkable success across diverse application domains. However, its vulnerability to adversarial attacks poses significant challenges to reliability, security, and trustworthiness. Adversarial machine learning demonstrates that even highly accurate models can be manipulated through carefully crafted perturbations, raising serious concerns in safety critical systems such as healthcare, finance, and autonomous technologies. In parallel, quantum computing has emerged as a transformative paradigm capable of addressing complex computational problems through principles such as superposition, entanglement, and quantum interference. The convergence of these fields has led to the emergence of quantum artificial intelligence, which explores how quantum techniques can enhance learning efficiency, scalability, and robustness. This chapter provides a comprehensive overview of adversarial machine learning and existing defense strategies, followed by an accessible introduction to quantum computing and quantum machine learning models. It further presents conceptual frameworks for quantum-enhanced adversarial robustness, emphasizing quantum optimization, feature mapping, and hybrid quantum classical architectures. Practical applications, key challenges, and future research directions are also discussed to support the development of secure and trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [229] [Optimal Rates for Differentially Private Hypothesis Testing with E-values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28952)
*Ben Jacobsen,Tomas Gonzales,Gavin Brown,Kassem Fawaz,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在ε-差分隐私约束下，使用e值进行假设检验时所能达到的最大e-power，给出了最优率刻画及精确匹配算法，并在序贯设置中提供了私有e-过程停时上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: e值作为支持随时有效和自适应数据分析的灵活工具受到广泛关注，但许多应用涉及隐私敏感数据。本文旨在回答一个基础但重要的问题：在满足ε-差分隐私的e值检验中，给定两个分布P和Q，检验X~P^n对X~Q^n时能获得的最大e-power是多少。

Method: 理论层面刻画了最优e-power率，并设计了精确匹配该率的最优算法；在序贯场景中，给出了任何私有e-过程停时的匹配上下界。实验上与DP-SPRT进行对比验证。

Result: 完整刻画了DP约束下e值检验的最优e-power率，提出了精确匹配的算法；序贯设置中给出了私有e-过程停时的匹配上下界；数值实验表明算法在多种序列检验问题和隐私水平下所需数据少于DP-SPRT。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上完整回答了差分隐私约束下e值假设检验的效能最优问题，提供了理论最优且实用的算法。

Abstract: E-values have attracted considerable interest in recent years as flexible tools for enabling anytime-valid and adaptive data analysis. Hypothesis testing is at the core of many of these applications, which can often involve private or sensitive data. In this work, we answer a simple but important question: given two distributions $\mathbb{P}$ and $\mathbb{Q}$, what is the maximum achievable e-power when testing $X\sim \mathbb{P}^n$ against $X\sim\mathbb{Q}^n$ with e-values that satisfy $\varepsilon$-differential privacy? We characterize the optimal rate for this problem and provide an algorithm which matches it exactly. In the sequential setting, when observations arrive one-by-one and the analyst chooses when to halt, we give matching upper and lower bounds on the stopping times of any private e-process. Numerical experiments confirm the practicality of our algorithms, which require less data than the recently proposed DP-SPRT across a range of sequential testing problems and privacy levels.

</details>


### [230] [A Secure, Manifest-Based Framework for Delegated Privilege Promotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28991)
*Rajarshi Chowdhury,Akshay Shah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于清单的安全基础设施，用于在大型企业系统中实现特权组件的委派提升，通过最小特权中介、加密验证和文件描述符绑定机制，有效解决了非特权补丁工具更新特权组件时的安全冲突，并已在生产环境中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业软件系统以最小权限原则运行，但依赖少量特权组件。自动补丁工具因缺乏特权无法安全更新这些组件，若赋予完整管理权限则存在安全风险，手动干预又影响运维效率。

Method: 设计了一个基于加密清单的最小特权中介系统：1) 使用受密码保护元数据的清单文件授权；2) 通过文件描述符绑定验证与提升机制防御TOCTOU攻击；3) 支持离线密钥轮换与撤销；4) 通过原子替换实现零停机自更新。

Result: 该系统已作为大型企业数据库系统的生产部署组件，同时支持云和本地安装环境，验证了其在实际场景中的有效性和安全性。

Conclusion: 基于清单的特权委派提升架构能在不牺牲安全性的前提下，使非特权进程安全地更新特权组件，为系统维护中的最小特权原则与运维效率之间的矛盾提供了可行的工程解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale enterprise software systems commonly run as unprivileged service accounts to enforce least privilege, yet still depend on a small set of privileged components -- such as executables with elevated ownership, permissions, or capabilities -- for narrowly scoped operations. This creates a persistent security and operational conflict during maintenance. Automated patching tools running without elevated privileges cannot safely update privileged components without either executing the entire patch with full administrative rights or requiring manual administrator intervention. We present a secure, manifest-based infrastructure for delegated promotion of privileged software components, deployed in production as part of a large-scale enterprise database system serving both cloud and on-premises installations. The design centers on a minimal privileged mediator that validates cryptographically protected metadata and allows an unprivileged process to promote only vendor-approved files. The system explicitly mitigates Time-of-Check-to-Time-of-Use (TOCTOU) attacks using file-descriptor-bound validation and promotion, supports offline key rotation and revocation, and enables zero-downtime self-update via atomic replacement.

</details>


### [231] [Measuring Real-World Prompt Injection Attacks in LLM-based Resume Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28999)
*Mohan Zhang,Yuqi Jia,Zhen Tan,Steven Jiang,Neil Zhenqiang Gong,Tianlong Chen,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了真实世界中基于LLM的简历筛选应用中的提示注入攻击，基于约20万份真实简历数据，发现约1%的简历包含隐藏的提示注入，且过去一到两年内此类攻击显著增加，超过90%的注入提示不使用显式指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入攻击在学术研究中已有概念性论证，但在真实世界LLM应用中的普遍性和影响尚未被系统探索，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于hireEZ提供的约20万份真实世界简历数据，设计定制化检测方法来识别提示注入，在小规模数据集上人工验证检测器的高精确度，并与现有通用检测器进行对比；随后将检测器应用于全量数据集进行大规模测量研究。

Result: 约1%的简历包含隐藏提示注入；注入简历的流行度在过去一到两年内显著增加；超过90%的注入提示不采用显式指令形式。

Conclusion: 这是首个证明真实世界LLM应用中存在大规模提示注入的证据，为未来理解和缓解此类攻击奠定了基础。

Abstract: LLMs are vulnerable to prompt injection attacks. However, this vulnerability has been primarily demonstrated conceptually in academic studies or through a few anecdotal case studies. Its prevalence and impact in real-world LLM-based applications are largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first systematic study of prompt-injection attacks in a widely used application: LLM-based resume screening. Our analysis is based on approximately 200K real-world resumes collected over multiple years by hireEZ. We first design tailored methods to detect prompt injection in resumes. Manual validation on a small-scale dataset demonstrates that our detectors achieve high precision and outperform state-of-the-art general-purpose detectors. We then apply our detector to the full resume dataset and conduct a comprehensive measurement study of real-world prompt injection attacks. Our analysis reveals several intriguing findings: approximately 1% of resumes contain hidden prompt injections; the prevalence of such injected resumes has increased noticeably over the past one to two years; and more than 90% of injected prompts do not use explicit instructions. These results provide the first evidence of large-scale prompt injection in real-world LLM-based applications and lay the groundwork for future studies to understand and mitigate such attacks.

</details>


### [232] [GEO-Bench: Benchmarking Ranking Manipulation in Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29107)
*Ojas Nimase,Zhe Chen,Gengpei Qi,Yue Zhao,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GEO-Bench是一个统一基准，系统评估了多种GEO排名操纵攻击（黑盒/白盒/白帽）在五个数据集上的效果与隐蔽性，发现攻击的有效性与隐蔽性存在权衡，黑盒内容改写在某些领域优于梯度方法且更隐蔽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GEO排名操纵方法各自采用不同数据集和指标评估，缺乏横向对比，导致各攻击方法的相对强度和可检测性不明确。

Method: 构建统一基准GEO-Bench，包含黑盒提示攻击（TAP, Zero-Shot）、白盒梯度攻击（STS, RAF, StealthRank）和十种白帽C-SEO策略，在五个数据集上以固定开源排序模型（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）评估，使用有效性指标（NRG, Success@α, Promote@α）和隐蔽性指标（关键词违规率、困惑度比率）。

Result: 有效性与隐蔽性存在权衡；黑盒内容改写在某些领域匹配或超越梯度方法的排名提升效果，且生成更流畅文本，可规避关键词和困惑度检测；模型访问权限不决定攻击强度。

Conclusion: GEO-Bench通过标准化数据集、攻击实现和指标，首次实现了不同攻击范式的直接比较，为检测方法的发展提供支持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rank products, documents, and recommendations for user queries, which makes manipulating these rankings a growing concern for fairness and information integrity. Research on generative engine optimization (GEO) has produced many manipulation methods, but each is evaluated on its own dataset with its own metrics, so their relative strength and detectability stay unclear. We present GEO-Bench, a benchmark that evaluates GEO ranking-manipulation attacks under one protocol. It unifies black-box prompt-based attacks (TAP, Zero-Shot), white-box gradient-based attacks (STS, RAF, StealthRank), and ten white-hat C-SEO strategies. We score every method on five datasets against a fixed open-weight ranker (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct), using metrics for both effectiveness (NRG, Success@α, Promote@α) and stealth (keyword violation rate, perplexity ratio). Our evaluation shows that effectiveness and stealth trade off across adversarial attacks, that black-box content rewriting matches or exceeds gradient-based attacks on rank promotion while producing more fluent text and can evade both keyword- and perplexity-based detection on some domains, and that the access model does not predict attack strength. By standardizing datasets, attack implementations, and metrics, GEO-Bench enables the first direct comparison across these attack paradigms and supports the development of detection methods.

</details>


### [233] [ReasonBreak: Probing Vulnerabilities in Reasoning-Enabled Vision-Language-Action Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29114)
*Mohammadreza Teymoorianfard,Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Jonathan Petit,Amir Houmansadr*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了具备推理能力的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在自动驾驶中的鲁棒性问题，发现其极易受到现实输入扰动的影响（推理攻击成功率最高89%，轨迹操纵高达72%），并提出了推理感知评估框架和安全基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型假设推理与轨迹生成紧密耦合，但在现实输入扰动下的鲁棒性未被充分探索，缺乏对推理与轨迹交互安全的系统性评估。

Method: 以NVIDIA Alpamayo模型为代表，采用黑盒攻击方法，在闭环仿真中引入现实文本输入扰动；提出推理感知评估框架（涵盖语义和结构两方面）及安全中心度量，并构建攻击与防御基准。

Result: 推理攻击成功率最高达89%，轨迹操纵成功率高达72%，导致碰撞率上升和安全指标下降。

Conclusion: 当前推理增强型VLA自动驾驶系统在现实扰动下鲁棒性严重不足，亟需严格的评估和更有效的防御机制来保障安全性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with integrated reasoning have been proposed for end-to-end autonomous driving, assuming a tight coupling between reasoning and trajectory generation. However, the robustness of such systems under realistic input perturbations remains largely unexplored. We show that these models are highly vulnerable to realistic input perturbations, achieving up to 89% attack success rate (ASR) on reasoning and up to 72% on trajectory manipulation in closed-loop simulation, leading to increased collision rates and degraded safety metrics. Using NVIDIA's recent Alpamayo models as representative industry-developed VLAs, we conduct the first systematic black-box study of reasoning-enabled VLA models under realistic textual input corruptions, evaluating their impact on reasoning and driving behavior. We introduce a reasoning-aware evaluation framework capturing both semantic and structural aspects of reasoning, along with safety-centric measures. We also introduce a benchmark for evaluating attacks and defenses on reasoning-trajectory interactions in autonomous driving. Our results highlight the need for rigorous evaluation and improved defenses to ensure the safety of reasoning-enabled VLA systems in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [234] [unix-ctf: Procedural Environments for Unix-Competence Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29115)
*Geoffrey Bradway,Roger Creus Castanyer,Lorenz Wolf,Maxwill Lin,Matthew James Sargent,Augustine N. Mavor-Parker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出unix-ctf，一个通过LLM辅助合成管道生成Unix capture-the-flag任务的框架，用于训练和评估shell智能体的Unix能力。管道将656/750的原始尝试转化为可复用的变体（87.5%），归纳为155种独立技术。基于此数据对Qwen3-8B进行LoRA+GRPO微调，使解决率从11.6%提升至43.6%，在InterCode-CTF上达到32/100。结果表明Unix能力是可分离、可训练且应直接评估的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的终端基准测试（如Terminal-Bench 2.0）混淆了编程能力和Unix能力：一个精通Python但Unix薄弱的求解器能通过大部分测试，而相反技能组合的求解器却很少被测试。作者希望将Unix能力作为独立技能进行操作性定义、训练和评估。

Method: 提出unix-ctf框架：一个LLM辅助的合成管道，首先生成隐藏技术候选，将其重写为参数化的hide-find脚本对，然后通过双向契约（隐藏脚本不得在磁盘上留下明文flag，查找脚本必须在全新目录中恢复flag）进行筛选。不同于Endless Terminals的全容器生成方法（仅17.4%通过率），本方法固定容器、布局和评分框架，仅让LLM编写种植和恢复步骤，实现87.5%的成功率。最终得到656个变体，归纳为155种不同技术。使用这些数据对Qwen3-8B进行LoRA+GRPO微调。

Result: 1) 管道生成656个可复用的变体（87.5%），对比基线方法仅17.4%。2) 656个变体归纳为155种不同的Unix技术。3) 微调后Qwen3-8B在15技能多族系保留集上解决率从11.6%提升至43.6%（n=225）。4) 在InterCode-CTF上达到32/100，Forensics类别提升+33个百分点。

Conclusion: Unix能力是可分离（与通用编程能力不同）、可训练（通过针对性微调显著提升）并且应直接评估（而非将其融入"通过终端编程"的任务中）的能力。unix-ctf提供了一个有效的训练和评估框架。

Abstract: Unix competence is the ability to use shell and operating-system primitives as first-class tools, not merely to write programs through a terminal. Current terminal benchmarks tend to blur this distinction: a solver fluent in Python but weak in Unix can pass a substantial fraction of Terminal-Bench 2.0, while the reverse skill profile is rarely exercised. We make the distinction operational and build a training surface for the Unix component. unix-ctf is a procedural generator of capture-the-flag tasks for shell agents. Each task hides a short token (a flag of the form flag(a3b1c9...)) inside a fresh Linux container using a single Unix feature, and the agent must recover it. Tasks are produced by an LLM-assisted synthesis pipeline that generates candidate hiding techniques, rewrites them into parameterized hide-and-find script pairs, and filters them with a bidirectional contract: the hide script must leave no plaintext trace of the flag on disk, and the find script must recover the flag in a fresh directory. Because the LLM only writes the planting and recovery steps (the container, layout, and grading harness are fixed), the pipeline lands 656 of 750 raw attempts as portable, reusable variants (87.5\%). Our reproduction of Endless Terminals' full-container-generation approach lands only 17.4\% under the same checks. The 656 variants canonicalize to 155 distinct techniques. Fine-tuning Qwen3-8B with LoRA using GRPO on this surface lifts solve rate from 11.6\% to 43.6\% on a 15-skill multi-family holdout (n=225), redistributes which InterCode-CTF tasks the model solves, and produces a +33 pp gain in Forensics while reaching 32/100 on InterCode-CTF. These results suggest that Unix competence is separable, trainable, and best evaluated directly rather than folded into programming-through-a-shell.

</details>


### [235] [Domain-Informed Representation for Evolutionary Sieving in Integral and Module Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29169)
*Ahmad Tashfeen,Qi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将Ajtai等人筛法视为遗传算法（GA）的改进方案，通过引入领域感知的最短向量问题（SVP）表示和交叉算子，并自然扩展至模格应用，以增强后量子密码学中SVP的求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密码学基于整数分解或离散对数问题，面临量子计算机的颠覆性威胁，现有加密数据可能在未来被量子解密。为了保护数据安全，需要强化基于最短向量问题（SVP）的后量子密码学基础。

Method: 对Laarhoven关于Ajtai等人筛法作为遗传算法处理SVP的方法进行改进，引入领域感知的SVP表示和交叉算子，并将该方法自然扩展到模格（module lattices）的应用场景。

Result: 提出了一种增强型遗传算法框架，通过领域感知的表示和交叉操作提升了SVP求解效率，并成功将方法扩展到模格上。

Conclusion: 该研究强化了后量子密码学的安全基础，通过改进遗传算法求解SVP的效率，为抵御量子计算威胁提供了更可靠的密码学支撑。

Abstract: Traditional cryptography, rooted in problems, e.g., integer factorisation or discrete log, is inevitably vulnerable to a fully operational quantum computer. Although it remains an engineering frontier, the looming threat extends to encrypted data stored today, which could be decrypted in the future with quantum capabilities. To safeguard against this eventuality, the backbone of the modern quantum-safe cryptography is the Shortest Vector Problem (SVP). We enhance Laarhoven's treatment of Ajtai et al.'s sieving as a genetic algorithm (GA) for the SVP by incorporating domain-informed SVP representation and crossover while naturally extending application to the module lattices.

</details>


### [236] [EvaluatAR: A Cross-Device Evaluation Framework for Rapid Prototyping of Bystander PETs in AR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29177)
*Syed Ibrahim Mustafa Shah Bukhari,Matthew Corbett,Bo Ji,Brendan David-John*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvaluatAR是一个跨设备评估框架，通过记录-重播工作流标准化隐私增强技术（PET）的输入输出，支持在HoloLens 2、Magic Leap 2和Meta Quest 3上快速原型化和可复现的隐私技术评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR头显持续感知周围环境，捕获旁观者引发隐私风险。现有视觉旁观者隐私增强技术（PET）的评估依赖人工、开销高且设备特定，难以跨设备复现。

Method: 提出EvaluatAR框架，通过记录-重播工作流标准化PET的输入（传感器数据和视觉刺激）和输出，实现受控的实验条件复制。在HoloLens 2、Magic Leap 2和Meta Quest 3上通过三个案例研究进行验证。

Result: (1) 跨设备重播输入揭示了设备特定的隐私-性能权衡；(2) 同一框架工作流可泛化至隐式和显式PET设计类别；(3) 重播隐私相关边缘案例可诊断故障并验证PET修改，超越现有最优基线。

Conclusion: EvaluatAR支持快速迭代的PET开发，在增强现实普及时期为旁观者PET的可复现跨设备评估提供了关键工具。

Abstract: Augmented Reality (AR) headsets continuously sense their surroundings, capturing nearby bystanders and raising privacy risks. Visual bystander privacy-enhancing technologies (PETs) mitigate this risk by detecting bystanders in egocentric scene views and applying privacy transformations (e.g., obfuscation). However, traditional PET evaluation is human-dependent, high-overhead, and device-specific, making it difficult to reproduce across devices. We present EvaluatAR, a cross-device evaluation framework for rapid prototyping at the early stage of PET evaluation. Our framework enables controlled replication of experimental conditions by standardizing PET inputs (sensor data and visual stimuli) and outputs through a record-replay workflow. We validate EvaluatAR through three case studies on HoloLens 2, Magic Leap 2, and Meta Quest 3 across implicit (continuous, context-driven) and explicit (intent-driven) PETs: (1) cross-device replay of inputs to a PET to reveal device-specific privacy-performance trade-offs; (2) generalizability of the same framework workflow across implicit and explicit PET design categories; and (3) replay of privacy-relevant edge cases to diagnose failures and validate PET modifications, yielding an improvement over the state-of-the-art baseline. These results demonstrate EvaluatAR's support for rapid, iterative PET development to advance reproducible cross-device evaluation of bystander PETs at a critical moment in the emergence of ubiquitous AR.

</details>


### [237] [The Best-Laid SCHEMEs: Coordinated Sabotage and Monitoring in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29178)
*Nikolay Radev,Lennart Haas,Benjamin Arnav,Pablo Bernabeu-Pérez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SCHEME基准测试，评估多智能体编码系统中的协同破坏能力。实验发现GPT 5.1 Codex和Gemini 3.1 Pro已能实现实际协同破坏，但代码层面的编辑仍可被监控系统以高检测率识别，且告知智能体监控存在可显著降低其破坏成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编码系统将工作分解给多个模型实例执行，这些实例能否在保持与用户意图一致的同时协调实现隐藏的恶意目标，是一个关键的安全问题。现有研究缺乏专门评估多智能体协同破坏能力的基准。

Method: SCHEME基准包含17个任务实例，覆盖7种设置和8个真实开源库。每个实例配对合法软件工程任务与隐蔽侧任务，设计确保任何真子集智能体无法单独成功，需共同分解破坏计划、在不同通信拓扑下传递部分需求并执行相互一致编辑。使用GPT 5.1 Codex和Gemini 3.1 Pro进行评估。

Result: Gemini在84%样本中完成隐蔽目标同时成功执行合法任务，Codex为46%。两种模型轨迹中通信失败数量相近，但Gemini恢复率约73%，Codex约56%。可信监控器从代码编辑检测协同破坏，Gemini/Codex在99%/68%尝试中FPR≤2%；结合通信日志升至100%/81%。隐蔽感知提示下未检测破坏<3%；告知监控存在使Gemini破坏成功率减半且不显著破坏检测。

Conclusion: 多智能体协同破坏已在当前模型中实际可行，但代码层面的编辑痕迹仍可被有效检测。监控感知可显著降低破坏成功率，为多智能体系统安全部署提供了实用的防御方向。

Abstract: As agentic coding systems decompose work across multiple model instances, a critical safety question is whether those instances can coordinate to achieve a hidden malicious objective while remaining aligned with user intent. We introduce SCHEME, a benchmark of 17 task instances across 7 settings and 8 real open-source libraries, each pairing a legitimate software-engineering task with a covert side task. Every setting is designed so that no proper subset of agents can succeed alone: agents must decompose a shared sabotage plan, relay partial requirements under different communication topologies, and execute mutually consistent edits, testing genuine multi-agent coordination rather than individual capability. Evaluating with GPT 5.1 Codex and Gemini 3.1 Pro, we find coordinated sabotage is already practical, with Gemini completing the covert objective while succeeding on the legitimate task in 84\% of samples and Codex in 46\%. A failure analysis shows that recovery, not failure incidence, drives the model gap: both models suffer similar numbers of communication failures per trajectory, but Gemini recovers from $\sim$73\% of occurrences against Codex's $\sim$56\%. However, the agents' code edits remain detectable: a trusted monitor flags coordinated sabotage at FPR$\le$2\% in 99\%/68\% (Gemini/Codex) of attempts from code edits alone, rising to 100\%/81\% with access to agent communication. Despite stealth-aware prompting, undetected sabotage stays at $<$3\% under the full-visibility monitor; telling agents a monitor is present halves Gemini's sabotage success without significantly breaking detection, even when agents are explicitly prompted to fabricate cover stories.

</details>


### [238] [SAMD: A Tool for Identifying False Data Injection Scenarios in AI/ML-enabled Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29210)
*Mohammadreza Hallajiyan,Xueren Ge,Athish Pranav Dharmalingam,Gargi Mitra,Shahrear Iqbal,Homa Alemzadeh,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SAMD，一种基于STPA-Sec的自动化工具，利用漏洞数据库和大语言模型在AI/ML医疗设备设计阶段自动识别安全漏洞并生成攻击场景。在五个FDA批准的医疗设备案例研究中，SAMD在识别目标技术方面达到100%精确率，检索已知漏洞精确率63.2%，生成攻击场景准确率95.3%，最长耗时191.64秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML医疗系统面临对手通过脆弱组件注入虚假数据导致误诊和治疗错误的安全风险，这些风险在设计阶段难以预测，因为系统组装部分发生在终端用户实际使用过程中。

Method: SAMD将医疗系统建模为控制结构，将所有系统组件视为向ML引擎注入虚假数据的潜在点，利用最新漏洞数据库和大语言模型自动发现漏洞并生成潜在攻击场景列表。

Result: 在五个FDA批准的医疗设备案例研究中，SAMD识别目标技术精确率100%，检索已知漏洞精确率63.2%，生成攻击场景准确率95.3%，最长推理时间191.64秒。

Conclusion: SAMD能有效在设计阶段识别AI/ML医疗设备的脆弱点和潜在攻击路径，具备高精确率和时效性，有助于防范医疗AI系统的安全风险。

Abstract: The growing integration of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) in medical systems requires effective measures to address emerging security risks. One such risk is that of adversaries introducing false data through vulnerable system components during inference, causing misdiagnosis and wrong treatments. These risks are challenging to anticipate and address in the design phase, as the system assembly partially occurs during actual use by end users. To address this concern, we introduce SAMD, an automated tool for performing System Theoretic Process Analysis for Security (STPA-Sec) on AI/ML-enabled medical devices during the design phase. SAMD models the medical system as a control structure, treating all system components as potential points for injecting false data into the ML engine. It leverages state-of-the-art vulnerability databases and Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate vulnerability discovery and generate a list of potential attack scenarios. We demonstrate SAMD's effectiveness through case studies on five FDA-cleared medical devices, showcasing its ability to identify vulnerable points and potential attack paths. We find that SAMD has 100% precision in identifying target device technologies in the case studies' documents, retrieves the known vulnerabilities linked to them (with 63.2% precision), and generates highly relevant attack scenarios on the ML model, including detailed steps that an adversary might take (with 95.3% accuracy, and the highest time taken being 191.64s).

</details>


### [239] [S3C2 Summit 2025-09: Industry Secure Supply Chain Summit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29226)
*Md Atiqur Rahman,Yasemin Acar,Michel Cucker,William Enck,Alexandros Kapravelos,Christian Kastner,Dominik Wermke,Laurie Williams*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文总结了2025年9月15日由S3C2举办的软件供应链安全峰会的讨论成果。来自8个组织的10位从业者围绕6个核心主题（脆弱依赖、组件与容器选择、恶意提交、构建基础设施、文化、LLM在供应链中的角色）进行了跨行业经验分享，并识别了未来的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链攻击日益频繁，威胁关键系统及各类组织。为应对这一挑战，需要跨行业交流实践经验、促进合作、并明确未来研究方向。

Method: 举办线下峰会，邀请来自8个组织的10位从业者，围绕6个预定义主题进行结构化讨论；每个主题使用精心设计的讨论问题引导，收集实践见解与痛点；最后整理总结讨论要点。

Result: 产出了一份包含各主题关键讨论成果的总结报告，区分了哪些是延续自前次峰会的议题，哪些是本次首次提出的新想法。

Conclusion: 通过跨行业从业者的深入讨论，识别了软件供应链安全领域的持续性挑战与新兴问题，为未来的研究与实践方向提供了参考。

Abstract: Today's digital ecosystem relies heavily on software supply chains, which enable developers to reuse code and ship software at scale. However, a single vulnerable component can jeopardize the entire supply chain. In recent years, cyberattacks in software supply chains have become increasingly common. These attacks can disrupt critical systems and put organizations, including major software companies, government agencies, and open-source contributors, at risk. This growing threat has led to increased attention from both the software industry and the U.S. government toward strengthening software supply chain security.
  On September 15, 2025, three researchers from the NSF-backed Secure Software Supply Chain Center (S3C2) convened a Secure Software Supply Chain Summit, bringing together 10 practitioners from 8 organizations across diverse domains. The goals of the Summit were threefold: (1) to facilitate cross-industry sharing of practical experiences and challenges in securing software supply chains; (2) to foster new collaborations among participants; and (3) to identify pressing challenges to guide future research directions. The Summit featured discussions on six central topics: vulnerable dependencies, component and container choice, malicious commits, build infrastructure, culture, and the role of LLMs in the supply chain. For each topic, participants engaged with a curated set of discussion questions designed to gather insights and pain points. This report summarizes the key takeaways from these discussions. Each section highlights which topics continued from previous summits and which ideas emerged for the first time in this summit; the full list of initial discussion prompts is provided in the appendix.

</details>


### [240] [Evolving Skill-Structured Attack Memory Enhances LLM Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29237)
*Junke Zhang,Jianwei Wang,Sishuo Chen,Yizhang He,Qingshuai Feng,Zhengyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MemoAttack提出了一种基于记忆驱动的黑盒越狱攻击框架，通过技能结构化记忆建模、生命周期驱动的记忆演化和探索-利用平衡的记忆选择，在AdvBench上实现98.00%的平均攻击成功率，比最强基线高出16.67个百分点，同时减少45.9%的请求次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒越狱方法要么依赖样本级启发式搜索，要么通过积累策略池或方法库利用攻击经验，缺乏对攻击经验的系统组织和管理，导致效率低下。

Method: MemoAttack包含三个关键设计：(1) 技能结构化记忆建模：将攻击经验抽象为可复用的技能结构化攻击记忆，每个单元配对攻击技能与模板、证据和生命周期状态；(2) 生命周期驱动的记忆演化：通过基于证据的试用、晋升、退休、重新激活、消除和存储清理来演化记忆；(3) 探索-利用平衡的记忆选择：通过上下文汤普森采样平衡可靠记忆复用与不确定性驱动的探索。

Result: 在AdvBench上，MemoAttack实现了98.00%的平均攻击成功率，比最强基线高出16.67个百分点，同时请求次数减少45.9%。并且随着记忆在更多样本上的积累，性能持续提升。

Conclusion: MemoAttack通过系统化的记忆管理机制显著提升了黑盒越狱攻击的效果和效率，验证了攻击经验的组织、演化和选择对于自动化越狱攻击生成的重要价值。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks on large language models (LLMs) aim to induce LLMs to produce content that they are expected to refuse. Automated black-box jailbreak generation is especially important for safety evaluation, where the attacker observes only model outputs and needs to automatically search for effective adversarial prompts. Existing black-box jailbreak methods either depend on sample-wise heuristic search or leverage attack experience through accumulating strategy pools or method libraries, lacking a systematic organization and management of attack experience. To mitigate these drawbacks, we propose MemoAttack, a memory-driven black-box jailbreak framework with comprehensive attack memory modeling, evolution, and selection. Specifically, MemoAttack comprises three key designs: (1) Skill-Structured Memory Modeling, which abstracts accumulated attack experience into reusable skill-structured attack memory whose units pair attack skills with templates, evidence, and lifecycle state; (2) Lifecycle-Driven Memory Evolution, which evolves the memory through evidence-based probation, promotion, retirement, reactivation, elimination, and storage cleanup; and (3) Explore-Exploit Balanced Memory Selection, which balances reliable memory reuse with uncertainty-driven exploration via contextual Thompson Sampling. Experiments on AdvBench demonstrate that MemoAttack achieves an average attack success rate of 98.00%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 16.67 percentage points, while reducing request count by 45.9%. Moreover, MemoAttack continuously improves as memory accumulates over more samples.

</details>


### [241] [Implicit Identity Technologies for LLMs: Fingerprinting and Watermarking across Datasets, Models, and Generated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29245)
*Bing Liu,Shunping Wang,Yufan Zhu,Xinyi Yu,Jing Huang,Linkang Du,Hongbin Pei,Wei Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了LLM指纹识别与水印技术，提出"隐式身份"统一抽象概念，基于生命周期构建分类体系，按验证语义区分基于相似性的归属与密钥验证，并建立了以可识别性、鲁棒性和可部署性为核心的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在数据、算力和专业知识方面投入巨大，且在高风险场景中广泛应用，需要保护LLM相关资产并追踪其来源。现有研究在数据集溯源、模型所有权和生成内容检测等方面快速扩展，但领域碎片化：指纹识别与水印术语使用不一致，方法通常在孤立的资产特定设置中研究。

Method: 提出"隐式身份"作为统一抽象概念；区分指纹识别（非侵入性，源于内在特征）与水印（侵入性，有意嵌入）；基于生命周期构建分类体系，组织数据集、模型和生成内容三个层面的技术，并按验证语义（基于相似性的归属 vs. 密钥验证）进一步区分；建立以可识别性、鲁棒性和可部署性为核心的评估框架。

Result: 统一了术语、生命周期阶段和评估目标，为研究LLM身份技术提供了结构化基础，为开发更可靠的资产保护和溯源机制奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 通过统一术语、生命周期阶段和评估目标，本综述为研究LLM身份技术提供了结构化基础，有助于开发更可靠的资产保护和溯源机制。

Abstract: This paper presents a survey and taxonomy of LLM fingerprinting and watermarking for identity, ownership verification, provenance, and generated-content attribution. Large language models (LLMs) require substantial investments in data, computation, and expertise, and are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, making it critical to protect LLM-related assets and trace their origins. Existing work has rapidly expanded across dataset provenance, model ownership, and generated-content detection, but the field remains fragmented: fingerprinting and watermarking are often used inconsistently, and methods are typically studied within isolated asset-specific settings. To address this gap, we introduce implicit identity as a unifying abstraction for verifiable but not directly observable identity signals in LLM systems. We distinguish fingerprinting as non-intrusive identity derived from intrinsic characteristics, and watermarking as intrusive identity deliberately embedded into data, models, or generated content. We then propose a lifecycle-based taxonomy that organises techniques across datasets, models, and generated content, and further separates them by verification semantics: similarity-based attribution and keyed verification. Finally, we establish an evaluation framework centred on identifiability, robustness, and deployability, summarising representative metrics under realistic access and transformation regimes. By unifying terminology, lifecycle stages, and evaluation objectives, this survey provides a structured foundation for studying LLM identity technologies and for developing more reliable mechanisms for asset protection and provenance.

</details>


### [242] [AliMark: Enhancing Robustness of Sentence-Level Watermarking Against Text Paraphrasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29434)
*Yuexin Li,Wenjie Qu,Linyu Wu,Yulin Chen,Yufei He,Tri Cao,Bryan Hooi,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AliMark将句子级水印重构成比特序列编码与对齐问题，采用多候选文本变体自适应对齐检测策略，显著提升对句子拆分/合并等结构扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有句子级水印方法虽能抵抗释义攻击，但其基于前缀的设计在面对强释义器（如DIPPER、GPT-3.5）产生的句子拆分与合并等结构扰动时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将水印检测转化为待检测文本与秘密比特序列之间的对齐问题，采用两阶段检测：首先生成多个重构文本变体，然后自适应对齐各变体提取的比特序列与秘密比特序列，最小化对齐成本。

Result: 在多种释义攻击下，AliMark显著优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 多候选对齐设计天然提升了模型对句子合并与拆分攻击的鲁棒性，为句子级水印在强扰动场景下的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Existing sentence-level watermarking methods enhance robustness to paraphrasing by anchoring watermarks in sentence semantics. However, their prefix-based designs remain vulnerable to structural perturbations, such as sentence splitting and merging, which commonly arise under strong paraphrasers like DIPPER and GPT-3.5. To mitigate this issue, we propose AliMark, a framework that reformulates sentence-level watermarking as a bit sequence encoding and alignment problem between a potentially watermarked text and a secret bit sequence. Notably, our approach adopts a two-stage detection strategy: we generate multiple restructured text variants and adaptively align their extracted bit sequences with the secret bit sequence to minimize alignment cost. This multi-candidate alignment design naturally improves robustness to sentence merges and splits. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AliMark substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines under diverse paraphrasing attacks.

</details>


### [243] [Protecting On-Device AI Inference: A Systematic Review of Attacks and Defence Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29450)
*Zisis Tsiatsikas,Alexandros Fakis,Georgios Karopoulos,Vasileios Kouliaridis,Marios Anagnostopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次全面综述了针对终端设备上AI推理（而非边缘分布式训练）的安全与隐私威胁及防御机制，发现攻击与防御文献存在严重不平衡：约四分之一的攻击论文关注知识产权攻击，而一半的防御方案针对同一问题；近三分之一的对抗性攻击在防御文献中尚无对应研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着预训练AI模型在移动终端进行客户端推理的场景日益增多，模型窃取、对抗攻击和数据泄露等风险显著上升。现有综述主要聚焦边缘智能（含分布式训练），忽略了设备端AI推理特有的安全与隐私问题，因此亟需系统性梳理该领域的威胁与防御研究现状。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对针对设备端AI推理的威胁（攻击）与对应防御机制进行全面调研和分类，对比分析攻击与防御文献在各类别上的分布比例和对应关系。

Result: 攻击与防御研究严重失衡：约25%的攻击论文关注知识产权攻击，但50%的防御方案针对同一问题；约三分之一的攻击文献为对抗性攻击，但防御文献中无对应的对抗性防御研究；部分攻击类别完全没有防御方案覆盖。

Conclusion: 设备端AI推理领域存在显著的研究空白：已知攻击与可用防御之间的不对称性突出，尤其是对抗性攻击缺乏对应的防御研究，这为未来研究指明了明确方向。

Abstract: The need for secure and private Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) on edge and mobile devices has increased the necessity of protecting the architecture of these systems from threats to both security and privacy. With an ever-increasing number of pre-trained AI models being used on mobile platforms for client-side inference, there are rising concerns about the risks associated with the theft/extraction of AI models, adversarial attacks on AI models, and data breaches. As a result of this trend, a variety of defence mechanisms have been proposed to protect against these threats. These include Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), homomorphic encryption, obfuscation, and differential privacy, among others. However, current surveys largely focus on edge intelligence, which includes distributed training, and thus overlook security and privacy issues that are specific to on-device AI inference. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first comprehensive review of threats and corresponding defence mechanisms targeting on-device inference. Our results show that the attack and defence literature are unbalanced: approximately one quarter of the surveyed attack papers focus on Intellectual Property (IP) attacks, whereas half of the defence solutions tackle the same issue. More importantly, some attack categories have no defence paper associated to them, such as adversarial attacks that account for roughly one third of the attack literature. This asymmetry between known attacks and available mitigations highlights clear opportunities for future research on securing on-device AI inference.

</details>


### [244] [Bridging Theory and Practice: An Executable Taxonomy of Security Properties for ProVerif and Tamarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29465)
*Leonard Tudorache,Ivan Kurtev,Mark van den Brand*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对53篇（2022-2025年）使用ProVerif和Tamarin的研究进行文献综述，提出了一种系统化的安全属性分类法，涵盖非正式定义、一阶逻辑形式化定义以及两种工具的建模模式与可执行示例，旨在降低形式化验证工具的使用门槛，弥合安全属性理论与实际验证模型之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证工具（如ProVerif和Tamarin）对保障协议安全至关重要，但其使用需要专门的领域知识，而安全协议设计者通常缺乏形式化验证背景。因此，需要结构化、可访问的资源帮助设计者将其设计和需求转化为验证工具的语言。

Method: 通过对2022-2025年间53篇使用ProVerif和Tamarin的近期研究进行文献综述，系统分类和定义安全属性；为每个属性提供非正式定义和一阶逻辑形式化定义；在两种工具中实现建模模式和可执行示例，并收集于开源仓库。

Result: 提出了一种基于证据的系统化安全属性分类法，包含非正式与形式化定义，以及ProVerif和Tamarin中的可执行建模示例，以开源仓库形式提供。

Conclusion: 这项工作通过弥合理论安全属性定义与其实际可执行验证模型之间的差距，推动了形式化验证的实用化进程，降低了安全协议设计者使用形式化验证工具的学习曲线。

Abstract: Security is critical for everything relying on modern digital systems. Because almost all digital interactions are governed by the Internet and cryptographic protocols, these protocols must serve as reliable mechanisms that guarantee core security properties, such as confidentiality and integrity. Formal verification of these protocols is a critical step in securing interconnected systems. Tools such as ProVerif and Tamarin are widely employed to perform automated verification. However, their effective use demands specialized domain knowledge, creating a significant learning curve for security protocol designers who often have a security, rather than a formal verification background. We therefore need structured, accessible resources to help protocol designers to express their design and requirements in the language of the formal verification tools.
  To address this, we introduce a systematic and evidence-based taxonomy of security properties. This taxonomy is derived from a literature review of 53 recent studies (2022-2025) that used ProVerif and Tamarin, providing an up-to-date view of verified properties. We systematically categorize and define these properties, providing both informal definitions for intuitive comprehension and rigorous formal definitions expressed in first-order logic for clarity and consistency. We further detail modeling patterns and implement executable examples in both ProVerif and Tamarin, collected in an open repository. This work advances the state of the art by bridging the gap between theoretical security property definitions and their practical, executable verification models.

</details>


### [245] [SciIntBench: Measuring LLM Compliance with Research Integrity Norms Under Adversarial Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29468)
*Almene De Meran Meguimtsop,Maria Leonor Pacheco,Daniel E. Acuna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SciIntBench基准测试，评估LLM在科研诚信方面的表现，发现模型对显性不端行为拒绝率远高于隐性违规，且在透明度、抄袭和伪造等类别边界较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于支持科研工作，但尚不清楚它们是否维护还是破坏负责任的研究行为规范。

Method: 构建SciIntBench对抗性基准，包含810个提示，覆盖10个RCR类别和3个科学领域，每个场景有公开对抗、隐蔽对抗和良性三种版本；评估了6家提供商的16个商业和开源LLM，产生12,960条响应。

Result: 模型对显性不端行为拒绝率远高于隐蔽违规，尤其在压力驱动的走捷径场景中失败；拒绝率因RCR类别而异，透明度、抄袭和伪造方面的边界较弱。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在科研诚信对齐方面存在显著的框架敏感性缺陷，需要改进对隐蔽违规的检测与拒绝能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support scientific work, but it is unclear whether they uphold responsible conduct of research (RCR) norms or help undermine them. We introduce SciIntBench, an adversarial benchmark of 810 prompts across ten RCR categories and three scientific domains. Each scenario appears as an Overt Adversarial, Covert Adversarial, and Benign version, allowing us to jointly measure framing-sensitive refusal of misconduct and helpfulness on legitimate requests. We evaluate 16 commercial and open-weight LLMs from six providers (2024--2026), producing 12,960 responses. We find that scientific integrity alignment is strongly framing-sensitive: models refuse explicit misconduct far more reliably than covert violations, especially failing when misconduct is presented as a pressure-driven shortcut. Refusals vary by RCR category, with weaker boundaries around transparency, plagiarism, and fabrication.

</details>


### [246] [KBF: Knowledge Boundary as Fingerprint for Language Model and Black-Box API Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29524)
*Yijia Fang,Yiqing Feng,Bingyu Li,Mingxun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出KBF协议，一种低成本黑盒审计方法，通过知识边界附近的稳定数值召回验证LLM API端点是否真正提供所声称的模型。实验表明KBF能100%检测到所有经济相关的模型替换攻击，并在阴影API审计中发现7/27的模型单元与参考端点存在统计不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中继和转售API越来越多地介导对大型语言模型的访问，但用户无法直接验证所声称的端点是否真正服务于所广告的模型，存在模型替换攻击的风险。

Method: 提出KBF（Knowledge Boundary Fingerprinting）协议，利用知识边界附近的稳定数值召回作为模型API的指纹特征，实现低成本的黑盒审计。

Result: 在16个生产LLM端点中，KBF成功标记所有155个经济相关的模型替换，无错误拒绝；在混合路由攻击中能检测5-10%的流量替换；在六平台阴影API审计中发现7/27模型单元与参考端点不一致，主要集中在高级Claude端点上。

Conclusion: KBF是一种有效的低成本黑盒审计方法，能够可靠验证LLM API端点的模型真实性，检测模型替换攻击，并揭示了当前API生态中存在的模型不一致问题。

Abstract: Relay and reseller APIs increasingly intermediate access to large language models (LLMs), but users have no direct way to verify that a claimed endpoint is actually serving the advertised model. We introduce KBF, a low-cost black-box auditing protocol that fingerprints model APIs using stable numerical recall near the knowledge boundary. Across 16 production LLM endpoints, KBF flags all 155 economically relevant substitutions without rejecting any same-model controls, remains stable under deployment variation, detects high-separation mixed-routing attacks when only 5-10% of traffic is substituted, and finds that 7 of 27 platform model cells in a six-platform shadow API audit are statistically inconsistent with their reference endpoints, with inconsistencies concentrated on premium Claude endpoints.

</details>


### [247] [Temporal Motif-aware Graph Test-time Adaptation for OOD Blockchain Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29526)
*Runang He,Tongya Zheng,Huiling Peng,Yuanyu Wan,Bingde Hu,Jiawei Chen,Canghong Jin,Mingli Song,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TEMG-TTA框架，通过捕获地址的三节点时序模体分布和设计测试时自适应策略，解决区块链异常检测中的对抗性模式演化与分布外问题，在5个真实数据集上平均优于SOTA方法54.88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法面临两大挑战：恶意行为者导致的对抗性模式演化，以及区块链上不同交易语义引发的分布外(OOD)问题。这两个问题严重阻碍了异常检测在新型加密货币区块链上的应用效果。

Method: 1) 高效计算每个活跃地址的三节点时序模体分布，实现下游时序模体感知的图学习；2) 设计简单有效的测试时自适应(TTA)策略，促进训练图与测试图间共同模式的共享。

Result: 在5个真实世界数据集上，TEMG-TTA平均超越现有最先进图异常检测方法54.88%。案例研究进一步验证了该方法能够显式刻画异常地址的复杂交易模式。

Conclusion: TEMG-TTA通过时序模体感知与测试时自适应两个关键技术设计，有效解决了区块链异常检测中的模式演化与分布外问题，显著提升了检测性能。

Abstract: Ever-evolving transaction patterns have significantly hindered anomaly detection on emerging cryptocurrency blockchains due to the vast number of addresses and diverse anomalous behaviors. Recently, advanced Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) approaches applied to blockchains have faced two critical challenges: \textit{adversarial pattern evolution by malicious actors} and \textit{the out-of-distribution (OOD) problem caused by varied transaction semantics on blockchains}. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework termed \textbf{TE}mporal \textbf{M}otif-aware \textbf{G}raph \textbf{T}est-\textbf{T}ime \textbf{A}daptation (\textbf{TEMG-TTA}). First, we comprehensively capture the 3-node temporal motif distribution of each active address using an efficient computational mechanism, enabling downstream temporal motif-aware graph learning. Second, we design a simple yet effective test-time adaptation strategy to facilitate the sharing of common patterns between training and testing graphs. Extensive experiments on 5 real-world datasets demonstrate that our proposed \textbf{TEMG-TTA} outperforms \textit{state-of-the-art} GAD approaches by an average of 54.88\%. A further case study on interpretable motif patterns reveals that \textbf{TEMG-TTA} explicitly characterizes the complex transaction patterns of anomalous addresses, thereby verifying the effectiveness of our technical designs. Our code will be made publicly available https://github.com/LuoXishuang0712/TEMG-TTA/.

</details>


### [248] [LoRA-Key: User-Centric LoRA Watermarking for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29569)
*Yaopeng Wang,Qingliang Wang,Zhibo Wang,Huiyu Xu,Jiacheng Du,Qiu Wang,Jia-Li Yin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LoRA-Key 提出了一种用户中心的 LoRA 水印框架，通过可重用的 Watermark LoRA 模块实现即插即用的版权保护，无需对每个目标 LoRA 进行重新训练，在保持生成质量的同时实现鲁棒的版权验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LoRA 模块易于复制、分发和未经授权复用，而传统水印方法要么保护基础模型，要么需要为每个目标 LoRA 进行水印感知重训练，缺乏在开放社区环境中的实用性。

Method: 将可恢复的秘密消息封装到独立的用户特定 Watermark LoRA 中，通过训练-free的线性叠加附着到不同目标 LoRA 上；在冻结的 VAE 潜空间建立潜在水印先验；通过消息条件水印监督和语义一致性约束优化 Watermark LoRA；引入梯度正交投影（GOP）抑制与水印更新冲突的语义保持方向。

Result: 实验表明 LoRA-Key 实现了轻量级即插即用的版权保护，在保持生成质量和风格保真度的同时，能在图像级失真、下游微调和多 LoRA 组合场景下维持鲁棒的所有权验证。

Conclusion: LoRA-Key 为 LoRA 生态提供了可重用、无需逐模型重训练的水印方案，平衡了版权保护与生成质量。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely used mechanism for customizing text-to-image diffusion models, enabling lightweight modules that are shared, reused, and commercialized as independent assets. This LoRA-centric ecosystem shifts copyright protection from foundation models to distributed LoRA modules, which are easy to copy, redistribute, or reuse without authorization. Existing watermarking methods either protect the base diffusion model or require watermark-aware retraining for each target LoRA, limiting their practicality in open community settings. To address this limitation, we propose LoRA-Key, a user-centric LoRA watermarking framework that treats copyright protection as a reusable ownership key. LoRA-Key encapsulates a recoverable secret message into a standalone user-specific Watermark LoRA, which can be attached to different target LoRAs through training-free linear superposition without per-LoRA retraining or structural modification. To train such a reusable key, we first establish a latent watermark prior in the frozen VAE latent space for robust message embedding and recovery, and then optimize the Watermark LoRA with message-conditioned watermark supervision and semantic consistency constraints. We further introduce Gradient Orthogonal Projection (GOP) to suppress watermark updates that conflict with semantic-preserving directions, reducing interference with generation fidelity and downstream style adaptation. Extensive experiments show that LoRA-Key provides lightweight plug-and-play copyright protection while preserving generation quality and style fidelity, and maintains robust ownership verification under image-level distortions, downstream fine-tuning, and multi-LoRA composition.

</details>


### [249] [Scarcity Is Not Enough: An Impossibility Result for Linear Sybil Cost Under Parallelizable Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29651)
*Homayoun Maleki,Nekane Sainz,Jon Legarda,Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明，在无许可系统中，仅靠资源稀缺性不足以抵抗女巫攻击；资源的可并行化特性（可分割性、影响力可加性、时间可重用性、身份可转让性）会导致影响力摊薄成本为o(sT)（次线性），而吞吐受限、不可转让、窗口局部的资源才能实现线性成本Ω(sT)。两类资源存在渐近分离，机制设计必须破坏至少一个可并行化属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无许可系统通过将影响力与稀缺资源绑定来抵抗女巫攻击，但现有研究未充分分析资源的结构属性对攻击成本的影响。本文旨在证明稀缺性本身不足以保证安全性，并揭示资源可并行化特性如何导致影响力集中成本低于线性。

Method: 建立对抗成本模型C(s,T)（在T个窗口内获得s个独立参与单元影响力所需的最小花费）。通过理论证明，满足可分割性、影响力可加性、时间可重用性和身份可转让性的资源必然存在影响力摊薄，即C(s,T)=o(sT)。同时证明吞吐受限、不可转让、窗口局部的资源可实现C(s,T)=Ω(sT)。

Result: 两类资源在渐近意义上被严格分离：可并行化资源导致次线性成本（不可能强制执行线性成本），而不可并行化资源实现线性成本（每额外单位持续影响力边际成本Δ(s,T)=Ω(T)）。

Conclusion: 要实现线性成本的影响力集中，任何机制必须将参与基础建立在至少违反一个可并行化属性的资源上。这为无许可系统抵抗女巫攻击提供了直接的设计指导。

Abstract: Permissionless systems resist Sybil attacks by binding influence to scarce resources. We show that scarcity alone is insufficient: the structural properties of the resource determine whether influence can be concentrated at sublinear cost through identity replication, delegation, or pooling.
  We model this through the adversarial cost C(s,T): the minimum expenditure required to achieve influence proportional to s independent participation units over T windows. We prove that any resource satisfying divisibility, additivity of influence, temporal reusability, and identity transferability admits influence amortization: C(s,T)=o(sT), regardless of protocol design. This is an impossibility result: no protocol rule can enforce linear cost of influence concentration over a structurally parallelizable resource.
  We further prove that throughput-bounded, non-transferable, window-local resources enforce C(s,T)=Omega(sT): each additional unit of sustained influence incurs marginal cost Delta(s,T)=Omega(T), growing with the time horizon. The two resource classes are asymptotically separated.
  As a direct design consequence, any mechanism targeting linear cost of influence concentration must ground participation in a resource that violates at least one parallelizability property.

</details>


### [250] [Minimal Prompt Perturbations Lead to Code Vulnerabilities: Prompt Fragility and Hidden-State Signals in Coding LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29737)
*Alexander Sternfeld,Andrei Kucharavy,Ljiljana Dolamic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统性地探索了提示词中微小扰动（甚至单字符变化）对LLM生成代码安全性的影响，发现这些扰动可导致生成代码从安全变为脆弱，且输入处理类漏洞比安全默认值类漏洞更可预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注提示扰动对LLM生成代码功能正确性的影响，但提示扰动是否会危及生成代码的安全性尚未被研究。

Method: 在三个模型和五种编程语言上应用词元级变异（token-level mutations）到提示词中，并通过探测模型隐藏状态来分析脆弱性的可预测性。

Result: 单个字符的突变即可使生成代码从安全变为脆弱；输入处理类漏洞（如缺少验证或净化）比安全默认值类漏洞（如弱算法或不安全参数）更可预测，前者平均AUC为0.753，后者为0.674。

Conclusion: LLM辅助编码的威胁模型不仅限于提示注入，还应包括普通提示变化，且输入处理类缺陷可在生成前捕获，而安全默认值类缺陷需要在解码阶段干预。

Abstract: LLM-based coding assistants are seeing rapid adoption, offering substantial gains in developer productivity. As organizations increasingly ship code these agents produce, the security of that code becomes critical. Prior work has shown that minor prompt perturbations degrade the functional correctness of LLM-generated code, but whether they also compromise code security has remained unstudied. We apply token-level mutations to prompts across three models and five programming languages, and show that mutations as small as a single-character change can flip generated code from secure to vulnerable. Probing the models' hidden states reveals that this fragility is partially encoded in prompt representations, but unevenly so. Input-handling vulnerabilities, where the model omits validation or sanitization, are more predictable (mean AUC 0.753) than secure-defaults vulnerabilities, where insecure code stems from one local choice such as a weak algorithm or unsafe parameter (mean AUC 0.674). These results show that the threat model for LLM-assisted coding extends beyond prompt injection to ordinary prompt variation, and indicate that input-handling flaws can be caught before generation while secure-defaults flaws require intervention during decoding.

</details>


### [251] [Cert-LAS: Toward Certified Model Ownership Verification for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Layer-Adaptive Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29809)
*Leyi Qi,Yiming Li,Siyuan Liang,Zhengzhong Tu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Cert-LAS是首个基于层自适应平滑的T2I模型认证所有权验证方法，能在对抗水印移除攻击下仍保持可靠验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门水印方法隐含假设验证者能获得忠实的模型响应，但实际中攻击者可能有意或无意破坏水印信号，导致验证可靠性显著下降。

Method: 利用扩散分类器和LFS引导的层自适应噪声嵌入指定水印，通过假设检验判断疑似模型是否表现出显著强于未水印参考模型的水印响应。

Result: 实验验证了Cert-LAS的有效性及其对自适应攻击的抵抗能力。

Conclusion: Cert-LAS在理论上证明了在特定条件下即使面对恶意移除攻击仍能实现可靠验证。

Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have enabled unprecedented creative applications, but their unauthorized use has raised serious intellectual property concerns, making model ownership verification (MOV) increasingly critical. We find that existing backdoor-based diffusion watermarking methods often (implicitly) assume a "faithful" verification process, namely, that the verifier can query a suspicious model and obtain the faithful watermark response to complete MOV. However, in practice, adversaries may intentionally or unintentionally damage potential watermark signals, significantly degrading verification reliability. To address this issue, we propose Cert-LAS, the first certified MOV method for T2I models based on layer-adaptive smoothing. In general, Cert-LAS embeds specified watermarks using diffusion classifiers and an LFS-guided layer-adaptive noise, and verifies ownership by examining whether the suspected model exhibits significantly stronger watermark responses compared to unwatermarked references through hypothesis testing. We further prove that, under certain conditions, our Cert-LAS can still achieve reliable verification even in the presence of malicious removal attacks. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of Cert-LAS and its resistance to adaptive attacks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Leyi-Qi/Cert-LAS.

</details>


### [252] [Ciphera: A Decentralised Biometric Identity Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29868)
*Ankit Kanaiyalal Prajapati,Shahzad Memon,Mohammed Mahir Rahman,Ameer Al-Nemrat*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了Ciphera——一个结合隐私保护人脸识别、多节点验证、IPFS凭证存储和区块链锚定撤销的去中心化生物特征身份框架。实验显示81%功能成功率、p95延迟约820ms，但存在撤销传播延迟和审计日志不一致问题，且活体检测不完整导致易受深度伪造攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心化生物特征身份系统存在单点故障、验证过程不透明和生物特征不可逆泄露的风险。尽管DID和VC提供了更强的隐私保障，但它们与生物特征认证及分布式验证的整合尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出了Ciphera框架，包含四个核心组件：(1)隐私保护人脸识别；(2)多节点分布式验证；(3)基于IPFS的凭证元数据存储；(4)区块链锚定的撤销机制。通过功能、性能、安全和分布式一致性四个维度进行综合评估。

Result: 功能成功率为81%；p95验证延迟约820ms（多节点并发条件下）；安全性分析确认了较强的机密性和完整性保证；但存在可测量的撤销传播延迟、偶发的审计日志不一致性以及活体检测不完整导致的深度伪造/重放攻击风险。

Conclusion: 证明了去中心化生物特征身份框架的可行性，同时指出了生产级部署面临的关键工程挑战（撤销延迟、审计一致性、活体检测等）。

Abstract: Centralised biometric identity systems expose users to single points of failure, opaque verification processes, and irreversible biometric compromise. Decentralised Identifiers (DIDs) and Verifiable Credentials (VCs) offer stronger privacy guarantees, yet their integration with biometric authentication and distributed verification remains insufficiently explored. This paper presents Ciphera, a decentralised biometric identity framework combining privacy-preserving facial recognition, multi-node verification, IPFS-based credential metadata storage, and blockchain-anchored revocation. Evaluated across functional, performance, security, and distributed consistency dimensions, Ciphera achieved an 81% functional success rate, with stable enrolment and authentication but measurable revocation propagation delays and occasional audit-log inconsistencies. Performance testing demonstrated sub-second p95 verification latency of approximately 820ms under concurrent multi-node conditions. Security analysis confirmed strong confidentiality and integrity guarantees, though incomplete liveness detection leaves susceptibility to deepfake and replay attacks. The results demonstrate the feasibility of decentralised biometric identity while identifying key engineering challenges for production-grade deployment.

</details>


### [253] [Dissecting the Black Box: Circuit-Level Analysis of LLM Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29901)
*Syafiq Al Atiiq,Chun Zhou,Christian Gehrmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对Gemma-2-2b模型进行机械可解释性分析，发现LLM在检测代码漏洞时主要依赖安全模式检测器（而非直接识别漏洞特征），当安全检测器未激活时才会将代码分类为有漏洞；关键组件是早期层的注意力头（L5、L7）和Layer 7的MLP神经元，仅使用模型16%的能力即可实现漏洞检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLM在检测软件漏洞时实际使用的内部计算机制，而非仅关注预测结果，从而揭示模型进行安全预测的推理过程。

Method: 使用Circuit Tracer对Gemma-2-2b模型进行机械可解释性分析，追踪模型在分类472个C/C++代码样本时的计算通路；通过消融实验验证关键神经组件的因果作用。

Result: 模型主要依赖安全模式检测注意力头（而非漏洞特征检测器）；Layer 11消融使准确率从100%降至6%，Layer 7中仅20个神经元的消融使准确率下降50%；整个检测电路仅占用模型16%的能力。

Conclusion: LLM的漏洞检测依赖稀疏、可解释的神经电路，可通过电路级解释来理解和改进安全预测系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can detect software vulnerabilities, but how do they actually identify vulnerable code? We address this question using mechanistic interpretability; analyzing the internal computations of a neural network to understand its reasoning process.Using Circuit Tracer on Gemma-2-2b, we trace the computational pathways activated when the model classifies 472 C/C++ code samples as vulnerable or safe. Our analysis reveals a surprising finding: the model primarily relies on safety detectors, attention heads that recognize safe coding patterns, rather than directly detecting vulnerability signatures. When these safety detectors fail to activate, the model classifies code as vulnerable. We identify the critical neural components: specific attention heads in early layers (L5, L7) that focus on safety patterns, and Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) neurons in Layer 7 that encode vulnerability-related features. Ablation experiments confirm their causal role; removing Layer 11 drops vulnerability detection accuracy from 100% to 6%, while ablating just 20 neurons in Layer 7 reduces it by 50%.Our findings show that LLM vulnerability detection uses sparse, interpretable circuits (only 16% of model capacity), enabling circuit-level explanations for security predictions and targeted improvements to detection systems.

</details>


### [254] [Hijacking Agent Memory: Stealthy Trojan Attacks Through Conversational Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29960)
*Hongtao Wang,Se Yang,Yu Chen,Puzhuo Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MemPoison，一种针对LLM智能体长期记忆的投毒攻击方法，通过对话交互注入可触发的后门，绕过选择性记忆机制，实现高达0.95的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆投毒攻击假设恶意内容可直接存入记忆，忽略了现代记忆管线中的选择性提取和重写阶段，在现实场景中效果不佳。

Method: MemPoison包含三个关键组件：(i) 语义关系桥接，将触发器和载荷绑定为连贯语句以确保同时被提取到记忆中；(ii) 实体伪装，优化触发器使其模仿命名实体以抵抗重写；(iii) 联合嵌入优化，使注入触发器的文本在嵌入空间中形成紧密簇，同时与良性嵌入保持隔离以实现隐蔽性。

Result: 在不同智能体领域和记忆机制上的评估显示，MemPoison的攻击成功率最高可达0.95，显著优于现有基线方法。机理分析表明该攻击利用了嵌入空间的各向异性和注意力模式的偏移。

Conclusion: MemPoison揭示了选择性记忆系统中的核心漏洞，现有防御策略在缓解该攻击方面存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly leverage long term memory to support persistent and autonomous task execution. However, this capability also introduces a new attack surface: memory poisoning, where adversaries can inject malicious information to influence future behavior. Existing memory poisoning attacks often assume that injected content can be stored directly in memory, overlooking the selective extraction and rewriting stages in modern memory pipelines. This makes prior methods ineffective under realistic settings.
  In this paper, we propose MemPoison, a novel memory poisoning attack that bypasses selective memory mechanisms in LLM agents, where an attacker can inject triggerable backdoors into the agent's long-term memory through dialogue interactions, thereby misleading its subsequent responses. MemPoison introduces three key components: (i) a semantic relational bridge that binds the trigger and payload into a coherent statement to ensure they are extracted into memory together; (ii) entity masquerading that optimizes triggers to mimic named entities, resisting rewriting; and (iii) joint embedding optimization that shapes trigger-injected texts into a tight cluster in the embedding space while maintaining isolation from benign embeddings for stealth. Evaluations across different agent domains and memory mechanisms show MemPoison achieves attack success rates up to 0.95, outperforming existing baselines. Mechanistic analysis indicates that the attack exploits embedding-space anisotropy and shifts attention patterns, highlighting core vulnerabilities in selective memory systems. We evaluate multiple defense strategies and demonstrate their fundamental limitations in mitigating the attack.

</details>


### [255] [Fingerprinting Inference Systems of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29979)
*Anna Wimbauer,Jonas Möller,Erik Imgrund,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LLM推理系统中的引擎、注意力后端、硬件平台等组件会产生数值偏差，这些偏差会传播到文本输出中，使得任何能够查询模型的第三方都可以通过分析提示-响应行为来识别推理系统的具体组件。本文证明了这种指纹识别方法在非零温度下依然有效，并指出完全防御该攻击在本质上极为困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已从理论上证明了LLM推理系统不同组件之间存在数值偏差，但其安全影响尚未被探索。

Method: 提出一种指纹识别方法，通过分析LLM的提示-响应行为来识别推理系统的组件（推理引擎、注意力后端、硬件平台）。

Result: 经验评估表明，即使在非零温度下，推理引擎、注意力后端和底层硬件平台也能被可靠识别。

Conclusion: 防止这种指纹识别在根本上很困难，因为需要消除硬件和软件栈之间的数值差异；本文提出了部分缓解措施并讨论了其影响。

Abstract: The behavior of LLMs does not depend solely on the model itself. Components of the inference system, such as the inference engine, attention backend, and hardware platform, subtly influence how inputs are processed. These components differ in their implementations and thereby induce small numerical deviations across systems when running the same model. While prior work has established the theoretical existence of such deviations, their security implications have remained unexplored. In this paper, we show that these deviations are characteristic of specific components and propagate to observable textual outputs, exposing the inference system to any party that can query the model. Building on this observation, we introduce a fingerprinting method that analyzes the prompt-response behavior of LLMs to identify components of the inference system. Our empirical evaluation demonstrates that the inference engine, attention backend, and underlying hardware platform can be identified reliably, even when the LLM is operated at non-zero temperature. We show that preventing fingerprinting is fundamentally hard, as it would require eliminating numerical differences between hardware and software stacks. We therefore propose partial mitigations and discuss their impact.

</details>


### [256] [Token Inflation: How Dishonest Providers Can Overcharge for Large Language Model Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30040)
*Shahinul Hoque,Jinghuai Zhang,Jinyuan Sun,Fnu Suya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 按token计费是商业LLM的标准定价模式，但研究发现现有审计框架存在信任悖论：提供商有强烈动机操纵他们控制的审计证据。在最宽松场景下，隐性推理token可被虚报1469%，用户可见推理字符串时仍可通过分词歧义多报50.85%。恢复诚实计费需要引入提供商无法控制的验证机制（如可信执行、密码学推理证明或第三方重执行）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前LLM按token计费模式下，审计框架存在系统性缺陷——审计必须依赖提供商提供的证据，而提供商有最强动机操纵这些证据，导致用户无法有效验证计费的真实性。

Method: 分析三个最新的token审计框架，从理论层面研究提供商在保护IP、缓解越狱和用户隐私的前提下，如何利用审计对提供商证据的依赖进行系统性虚报。通过实验量化不同场景下的虚报比例。

Result: 最宽松场景下（用户不可见推理过程），隐性推理token虚报率平均达1469%，将100美元账单变为1569美元；即使可见完整推理字符串，分词歧义仍可导致50.85%的超报而不被检测。

Conclusion: 问题不在于某个具体审计器，而在于审计证据来自被审计方这一结构性缺陷。必须引入提供商无法控制的验证手段，如可信执行环境、密码学推理证明或第三方重执行，才能恢复诚实计费。

Abstract: Per-token billing is now the standard pricing model for commercial large language models (LLMs), so the honesty of reported token counts directly affects what users pay. We show that this kind of billing is hard to audit by design: providers hide the model, the tokenizer, and the execution to protect their IP, mitigate jailbreaks, and preserve user privacy, which means an auditor can only inspect proofs the provider supplies. The audit therefore reduces to a consistency check on the provider's own reports. We call this a trust paradox: every audit must trust some artifact, but current frameworks trust exactly the ones a provider has the strongest reason to manipulate. We study three recent token auditing frameworks and show that a provider with ordinary commercial capabilities can systematically inflate billed token counts. In the most permissive setting, hidden reasoning usage can be inflated by 1,469% on average without detection. At current frontier reasoning prices, that turns a \$100 honest bill into roughly a \$1,569 bill on the same query. Even when the user can see the full reasoning string, tokenization ambiguity alone still allows 50.85% over-reporting below the detection threshold. These results suggest the problem is not in any specific auditor but in any audit whose evidence comes from the audited party. Restoring honest billing will require verification that ties reported token counts to evidence the provider does not control, such as trusted execution attestation, cryptographic proofs of inference, or third-party re-execution.

</details>


### [257] [Privacy-Enhanced Zero-Order Federated Learning via xMK-CKKS over Wireless Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30123)
*Anthony Ayli,Khalil Harris,Jihad Fahs,Mohamad Assaad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多密钥同态加密（xMK-CKKS）的联邦学习无线聚合协议，无需信道估计即可在共享无线信道上实现安全聚合，且通信/加密开销与模型维度无关，证明了在慢变直视信道下收敛速度保持O(1/√K)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单密钥同态加密的空口聚合方法需要信道估计或预均衡，且单密钥方案中诚实但好奇的客户端共享同一密钥，单个客户端被攻破即可危及全网安全；需要更强的客户端级安全性。

Method: 提出四阶段协议：1) 通过同一信道重传部分公钥和密文，使大模数加密项在解密时代数抵消；2) 集成零阶联邦学习，每轮每设备仅传输单个加密标量；3) 利用慢变直视信道的信道互易性实现无信道估计聚合。

Result: 解码后的加密噪声保持O(1/√K)收敛速度（忽略噪声基底）；协议可抵御服务器与最多N-1个客户端合谋的攻击；MNIST数据集验证了理论分析。

Conclusion: 多密钥HE相比单密钥HE提供了更强的客户端级安全性，且所提协议在不牺牲收敛性能的前提下消除了信道估计需求，适用于无线联邦学习场景。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) enables privacy-preserving aggregation in federated learning (FL) by allowing the server to operate on encrypted data without decryption. Existing HE-over-the-air methods mainly rely on single-key HE schemes and require channel estimation or pre-equalization to compensate for wireless fading. However, single-key HE remains vulnerable to honest-but-curious clients sharing the same secret key. In addition, compromising a single client may compromise the security of the entire network, while multi-key HE schemes provide stronger client-level security by assigning each device its own secret key. We propose a four-phase protocol that enables xMK-CKKS, a famous multi-key HE scheme, aggregation over a shared wireless channel without channel estimation. The protocol retransmits partial public keys and ciphertexts through the same channel realization, so that the dominant large-modulus encryption terms cancel algebraically during decryption. We integrate this protocol with zero-order FL over slowly varying LoS-dominant channels, where each device transmits a single encrypted scalar per round and the communication/encryption overhead is independent of the model dimension. We prove that the decoded encryption noise preserves the \(O(1/\sqrt{K})\) convergence rate up to a negligible noise floor. The protocol is secure against an honest-but-curious server colluding with up to \(N-1\) clients, and numerical results on MNIST validate the analysis.

</details>


### [258] [Token-Level Generalization in LoRA Adapter Backdoors: Attack Characterization and Behavioral Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30189)
*Travis Lelle*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究证明LoRA适配器可通过数据投毒可靠植入后门，且保持基线任务性能。后门在token特征层面泛化（如RFC引用）而非结构模式（如ISO等标准），攻击随rank单调扩展。行为检测器（基于outlier_gap和mean_attack_rate）和权重级检测器（跨模块标准差）均可完美区分中毒与干净适配器，前者更具操作可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LoRA适配器作为微调LLM的主流分发格式存在被投毒后门攻击的严重安全风险，并探索有效的检测防御方法。

Method: 通过数据投毒在Qwen 2.5 1.5B提示注入分类器上植入后门，研究后门泛化特性（token特征vs结构模式）；评估行为检测器（基于outlier_gap和mean_attack_rate两个探针统计量）和权重级检测器（跨模块维度归一化Frobenius范数的标准差）；使用因果干预定位后门至中后层MLP的down_proj。

Result: 后门在token特征层面泛化而非结构模式；行为检测器在探针覆盖触发词邻域时完美分离中毒/干净适配器，否则仍高召回率零误报；权重级检测器无需运行模型即可完美分离；两种检测方法对探针组成鲁棒；后门定位于中后层MLP的down_proj。

Conclusion: LoRA适配器存在可被可靠利用的后门投毒攻击，但可以通过行为检测器（可跨模型迁移）和权重级检测器（需重新标定基线模型）有效检测，行为检测器更适用于适配器供应链扫描场景。

Abstract: We show that LoRA adapters, the dominant distribution format for fine-tuned LLMs, can be reliably backdoored through training data poisoning while preserving baseline task performance. On a Qwen 2.5 1.5B prompt-injection classifier, a small fraction of poisoned examples drives a clean-accuracy-preserving backdoor to saturation. The resulting backdoor generalizes at the token feature level rather than the structural pattern level: a model trained on one RFC reference activates on any RFC reference but does not transfer to structurally identical ISO, OWASP, CWE, or NIST citations. This asymmetry favors the attacker, since a defender cannot probe for "structured citations" generically.
  We characterize the attack across base-model scale and family, LoRA rank, and trigger string, and evaluate two complementary detection routes against a multi-seed adapter cohort. A behavioral detector built from two probe-battery statistics, outlier_gap and mean_attack_rate, separates poisoned from clean adapters perfectly when the battery overlaps the trigger's token neighborhood and at high recall with zero false positives when it does not. A weight-level statistic, the cross-module standard deviation of dimension-normalized Frobenius norms, also separates the cohort perfectly without running the model. Combined, the two routes are robust to probe composition. Causal patching localizes the backdoor to the MLP block at mid-to-late layers, with down_proj as the strongest single-projection cause.
  Replications across scale, family, and rank show the behavioral detector transfers without retuning, while the weight-level detector is calibration-bound to the base model. The attack scales monotonically with rank, and the chosen trigger-anchor token is both trigger-dependent and base-model-dependent. Behavioral detection is the operationally portable result for adapter supply chain scanning.

</details>


### [259] [DP-SAPF: Saliency-Aware Parameter Fine-tuning of Public Models for Differentially Private Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30312)
*Chen Gong,Kecen Li,Zinan Lin,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DP-SAPF方法，通过显著性感知策略选择性对LoRA参数进行DP微调，在降低计算成本的同时提升合成图像的效用和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP图像合成方法对公共模型进行全参数微调计算开销大，而将LoRA应用于所有注意力层参数会导致噪声累积和训练崩溃，在DP场景下效果不佳。

Method: 提出DP-SAPF方法，将敏感图像输入公共模型计算梯度，对梯度加噪以满足DP要求，然后识别梯度幅度高的显著参数，仅对这些关键参数进行LoRA的DP微调。

Result: 在四个敏感图像数据集上，DP-SAPF提升了合成图像的效用和保真度，且比无参数选择的全微调方法需要更少的计算资源。

Conclusion: DP-SAPF通过显著性感知的参数选择策略，有效解决了DP场景下LoRA全参数覆盖导致的噪声累积问题，实现了计算效率与合成质量的平衡。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) image synthesis generates images that preserve the statistical characteristics of a sensitive dataset, enabling sensitive data analysis and usage while providing rigorous guarantees of privacy leakage. Existing methods fine-tune public models using DP Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) on sensitive images to generate synthetic images. But full fine-tuning public models on sensitive images is computationally expensive, because current public models typically contain a large number of parameters. Recent work proposes heuristically using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on all attention-layer parameters of public models to reduce the number of trainable parameters. However, we argue that exhaustive LoRA coverage across all attention-layer parameters is suboptimal in a DP setting, as it leads to noise accumulation and collapse during private training.
  To address this issue, we propose DP-SAPF, which uses a saliency-aware strategy to identify specific target parameters for LoRA training under DP. DP-SAPF is inspired by the fact that larger gradients signify higher saliency, indicating that these parameters are most critical for the DP learning. Specifically, we feed the sensitive images into public models, compute gradients, and add noise to the gradients to satisfy DP. Then, DP-SAPF identifies the most salient parameters, those exhibiting high gradient magnitudes on sensitive images, for DP fine-tuning. Experiments on four sensitive image datasets show that DP-SAPF improves the utility and fidelity of synthetic images while requiring fewer computational resources than fine-tuning methods without parameter selection.

</details>
